## 3. One ORIGINAL theorem target and its eight exact welds

**Goal:** Construct an actual 5D Lorentz Einstein solution on the two
physical material faces, `Ric(g)=-4g`, with a common induced timelike
wall, physically sourced Israel `[K]-h tr[K]+3j h-S=0`, appropriate
harmonic gauge, positive lapse/spatial geometry, spacelike wall normal,
reflecting weighted AdS exterior, and the nonzero Hopf spatial initial
data from BN. Preserve the periodic normal geometry and all scalar,
vector, Weyl and normal-holonomy modes. No claim that this final theorem
is currently proved or accepted.

1. **W0 – initial-data injection, not re-solve (BR79, P0BN/BO/BQ).**
   Provide an actual inhabitant of `OriginalP0BNTimeSymmetricInput`
   with R(h)+12=0, initial K=0 and the genuine *same h* and same
   spatial shell. Attach BE lapse Hermite, BQ fourth normal source,
   initial harmonic constraints and wall timelike-corner regularity.
   Required output: a 5D-compatible initial/boundary corner certificate
   of sufficiently high regularity, NOT a new scalar barrier.
2. **W1 – physical background/normal source identity (BR48–72).**
   Establish that the BR71/72 optical 30+10 D/E rows are the full
   Fréchet derivative of the **actual** nonlinear two-face
   `h,K,C` geometry with BR70 physical tangent/normal frame,
   BR65/66 source warp-acceleration and mean, BR67/69 higher time
   jets, BR74/75 ambient curvature, and original choice of lapse and
   density. Proof must be bidirectional and preserve nonzero
   graph/connection source; equality is not automatic from counts.
3. **W2 – normal-frame multiplier and differentiated energy (BR80).**
   Use the exact physical ADM multiplier current with its deformation
   work, BR43 H¹ two-sided trace premium, BR47 order-eight boundary
   source, BR76/77 moving flux with tilt, and BR41 signed wall work.
   Construct a real positive (not complex bilinear) weighted
   first-order 15+5 symmetrizer and prove its high-order commutator

   artificial coordinate-shift bound but does not make the boundary

4. **W3 – actual glancing/Lopatinski closure (BR33/34/37, BR82).**
   Prove the full variable D or E physical boundary map yields a
   uniform or **quantified tame** trace estimate in damped sectors.
   BR37 establishes only a source-specific frozen inverse with
   glancing chart losses. A stronger estimator may be found through
   principal normal characteristic geometry or a symmetrizer

   frequency and derivative costs; do not assume `M^4/M²` bounded.
5. **W4 – physical subsidiary propagation, NOT a projected guess.**
   Weld BR55 contracted curved Bianchi source and BR78
   tension/matter Ward to BR74/75 full **curved ambient** Codazzi/Gauss
   and BR29 original 35D/nine-speed boundary carrier. Prove the
   physical source lies in the actual moving projected kernel under

   Distinguish 5 harmonic channels from 9 Weyl zero modes.
   Preserve BR29 rank12 failure of a selected constant wall-only
   Gram as negative control.
6. **W5 – weighted reconstruction modulo exactly characterized kernel.**
   Reuse BS10–13/BR31/57 *forced normal* curvature-to-shear

7. **W6 – nonlinear iteration with derivatives and constraints paid.**

## 5. BR83–84 — full 5D current and original BN/BE timelike-wall corner

This second phase strengthens the **actual operator**, rather than adding generic carrier records.

**BR83 `scripts/check_hopf369_br83_full_5d_energy_current.py`:**
The source-independent classical differential identity behind BR80 is now
transcribed and independently checked on a genuine **five-axis** ADM
Riemannian-spatial block with non-diagonal positive 4×4 spatial H,
four nontrivial ADM shift components, independent first jets in all
five directions of N,H,v, and symmetric scalar Hessians. The full
contravariant stress `T=grad u tensor grad u -g^-1 g^-1(du,du)/2`,
future slice normal `X=(1,-v)/N`, and densitized current
`J=D T^mu{}_nu X^nu` are constructed **from the original ADM
metric**, including its actual derivative jets. The exact identity
being checked is
```
 ∂mu J^mu / D =
      (□_g u) X(u) + (1/2) T^{mu nu}(Lie_X g)_{mu nu}.
```
No scalar frozen coefficient is substituted for the true time,
spatial/normal lapse, shift, H-shear and Christoffel/deformation
terms. The checker has 15+5 independent original field-channel
stress evaluations, true moving material-wall flux and a nonzero
deformation negative control. It provides a genuinely five-dimensional
source **principal** energy geometry, but remains
COMMITTED_PENDING_LOCAL_RUN. It does not assert the full 15-coupled
Einstein connection is symmetric-hyperbolic or that the 30+10 physical
boundary map is dissipative.

**BR84 `scripts/check_hopf369_br84_bn_wall_corner.py`:**
The existing BN/BE/P0AK spatial/wall sources give a **complete
time-symmetric initial wall Israel corner** under the explicitly
prescribed zero-shift/static-material initial gauge, not a generic
positive-time boundary theorem. For the actual four-dimensional
timelike wall induced metric `h4=diag(-N²,H_3)`, positive N and
positive non-diagonal H_3, its physical wall second fundamental form
is
```
 Kwall_tt=-N ∂normal N,
 Kwall_AB=1/2 ∂normal H_AB .
```
Thus all ten original spacetime-wall Israel equations
`[Kwall_ab]=j h4_ab` are **bidirectionally equivalent** to
```
 [Kwall_AB]=j H_AB       (six existing spatial BN/BE components)
 [∂normal log N]=j       (one BE/P0AK lapse-Hermite corner)
 [Kwall_tA]=0            (three time-symmetric mixed corner conditions).
```
The last three are actual zero-shift/time-symmetric material **gauge
conditions**, not consequences of the scalar Hamiltonian. The
full four-dimensional trace reverse is
`[K]-h tr[K]=-3j h`; BR63's full Israel inverse recovers the jump
without a Fourier denominator. The checker retains mismatched-lapse
and mixed-velocity negative controls. For arbitrary initial shift,
wall motion or nonzero Cauchy K, the genuine additional mixed-corner
equations must be paid separately.

## 7. BR89–91 — warped adapted inverse and same-metric defect source

Reuse BR37 (existing exact Lie-algebra and finite-chart intertwiner),
BR34 actual warped DtN, BR35 rest-frame Green contraction, BR63/78
Israel and Ward, BR29 original Weyl carrier. Do not reopen those owners.

**BR89 / W3: positive adapted frozen reference norm.** In the scaled
D row coordinates let T transport all D rows, U transport endpoints,
D(z) U = T D(ell). For E, reorder its replacement row into the
omitted temporal D slot r. Let g0 be the scalar replacement row
at rest, l=g0 D(ell)^-1 and v=T^-1 e_r. Rotational covariance
gives l(v)=a l(e_r), a=s/ell. With f0 the E data with its omitted
D slot set to zero and g the replacement datum, the full inverse
is exactly
  y=T^-1 f0; lambda=(g-l(y))/(a*l(e_r));
  u=U D(ell)^-1 (y+v lambda).
This is the existing BR37 rank-one update performed in the **rest**
data and endpoint norm, not a new physical allocation. In positive
Hermitian endpoint norm ||U^-1 u||, D is bounded by its rest constant;
E satisfies
  ||U^-1 u|| <= ||D(ell)^-1|| [||y||+
     max(1,|xi|/|s|) (|g|+||l|| ||y||)/|l(e_r)|]
using spatial rotation to align xi. This is conditional on the
existing BR35/37 rest inverse and nonzero rest replacement response.
No product of two additional large Lorentz matrix norms is necessary
in this adapted norm. It is frequency dependent; it is NOT a local
physical energy or uniform Sobolev norm. BR87 physical coordinate
powers and the E low-temporal pole remain valid. New checker uses
actual BR34 warped collocation (three frequency sectors, both fields)
and tests full two-sided inverse, not just one forced solution.
Numerical checks pending; require interval or analytic DtN acceptance
for any continuum promotion. Variable localization needs estimates
of U, T, their derivatives and commutators, and the exact physical
boundary source; no claim that this is already paid.

**BR90 / W4: full defect-aware Gauss–Israel jump.** Use fixed signed
j=-6/5, matched h, [K]=jh, mean M, D=Ric+4g. Covariant Codazzi
gives [D_na]=0 for constant tension. Scalar Gauss gives
  [D_nn]=tr_h[D_ab]+6j tr_h M.
Tangential Riemann jump is -j(h wedge M); mixed jump vanishes;
electric jump is [D_ab]+j(2M+h tr M). Subtract g wedge the
actual Schouten defect (D-g tr D/8)/3 to reconstruct all 55 packed
curvature slots. The result is algebraically Weyl and the Ricci
contraction is the original defect. Its tracefree M component is
exactly j times the existing BR29 jump map; the pure trace mean
cancels only after the scalar Gauss relation has been paid.
The defect-to-Weyl map has rank 9; its P0 projection rank 6;
An projection rank 8. P0 kernel on the ten tangent defect channels
is four-dimensional: the three time-space mixed entries and
pure Lorentz trace. Hence even physical matched Israel with
constant tension does not algebraically remove the zero-speed
defect. Do not equate these ranks to evolution-mode counts.

**BR91 / W4: actual differential Cotton source.** At an orthonormal
point retain all 75 covariant first jets of D. Contracted Bianchi
imposes five independent equations, leaving a 70-dimensional jet
space. Differentiate the Schouten defect and use BOTH Weyl divergence
and full differential Bianchi to construct electric and magnetic
forcing in BR29's actual 100-slot embedding. Compress by G^-1 X^T.
The resulting compatible Cotton map has rank 25 and its projection
onto P0 has rank 9. The ranks remain 25/9 on the smaller image of
D=S(C) constructed from actual symmetric Hessians of the five gauge
components with all five homogeneous principal wave constraints.
The exact contraction identity on that Hessian carrier is
  div S(C) - 1/2 d tr S(C) = Box C/2.
Thus no unconditional P0 Cotton cancellation is available even on
wave-compatible gauge jets. The proper proof order is:
  (i) construct the reduced metric using actual W1–W3;
  (ii) prove the homogeneous gauge wave IBVP with zero initial and
       compatible physical boundary data on that same reduced metric;
  (iii) infer D=0 for Ered=0; then the Cotton source and defect jump
        vanish and BR29 vacuum subsidiary identities apply;
  (iv) if evolving independent Weyl variables, separately prove
       their equality to geometric curvature using the coupled
       subsidiary flux/trace estimate. Preserve BR29 rank12
       obstruction to the chosen separated wall-only Gram.
This is not a no-go for the final physical theorem, but a no-go
for using contracted Bianchi alone as a projected-source-zero proof.

## 8. BR92–95 — same-metric subsidiary, signed D fold and glancing derivatives

BR26/BR5 already prove gauge uniqueness for the old six-value allocation,
but BR32 proves its reduced metric principal problem rank deficient.
BR33 fixes this with physical mean force and different D/E conditions.
Do not import BR26 energy into D or E unchanged. Reuse BR33/68/70/86/89.

**BR92 (W4a):** Ered=Ric+4g-S(C), S=symgrad C. The half trace reverse
TR(Ric+4g) is divergence free in five dimensions. Metric compatibility
and the covector commutator give
  div(S(C)-(1/2)g div C)=(Box C+Ric(C))/2,
  Box C+Ric(C)=-2 div TR(Ered).
Use the actual reduced metric Ricci coefficient, not -4 before C=0.
Correct BR55's misleading full-trace comments (native traceReverse5
already uses half). Exact non-diagonal Lorentz 2-jet oracle computes
Christoffel, its derivatives, Ricci, 125 covariant Hessian entries and
five equations, with both incorrect shortcuts explicitly refused.

**BR93 (W4a):** In a Gaussian collar with common induced h, [K]=jh,
constant j, tr_h M=0, fold periodic n in [0,L] to r in [0,L/2].
Write p=C(r), v=R C(L-r), R=diag(1,1,1,1,-1), A=p-v, B=p+v.
Use actual reflection differential in non-Gaussian charts. Thus
A_a=[C_a], A_n=C_n^++C_n^-, B_a=C_a^++C_a^-, B_n=[C_n].
Radial jets A_r=C'_++R C'_-, B_r=C'_+-R C'_-.
BR33's exact physical derivative rows become
  B_{a,r}+D_a B_n-2 M_a^b A_b-j B_a=0,
  A_{n,r}-D^a A_a-2j A_n=0.
BR68's physical correction from -j/2 is retained. D value conditions
A_a=0, A_n+B_n=0; E instead A_i=0,A_n=B_n=0, no A_0 condition.
At smooth midpoint, A_a=0, A_{n,r}=0, B_n=0, B_{a,r}=0 by parity.
The full signed fold and its inverse are exact, no frequency division.

**BR94 (W2/W4a): conditional analytic uniqueness proof.**
Assume a GIVEN regular reduced solution with reflection-invariant
metric and therefore reflected full covector wave/Ricci operator,
positive lapse/coercive spatial metric, bounded first/lower coefficients,
constant j, D physical BC and zero initial C and time derivative.
Use boundary-adapted slice/time multiplier tangent to wall and midpoint
with normal spatial there, and homogeneous nonincoming exterior flux;
noncompact domain additionally requires justified weighted integration
and trace bounds. Reflection invariance is not asserted for nonzero BN.
Naturality makes A and B independent homogeneous normally hyperbolic
systems. A has four Dirichlet components and A_{n,r}=2j A_n at wall,
plus the above midpoint homogeneous D/N conditions. The BR86 positive
component/fibre energy (normal/tangent block diagonal at wall) plus mass
has Robin outward power -2j weighted A_n partial_t A_n; add the signed
boundary potential j times the weighted A_n squared. Weight derivatives
yield only quadratic trace work. Treat covector connection terms as
bounded lower-order coefficients on this given geometry. Dirichlet
velocities and Neumann derivatives contribute zero flux.
For a>0, epsilon>0,
  |u(0)|² <= epsilon int_0^a |u'|² +
              (1/a+1/epsilon) int_0^a |u|²
by averaging u(0)^2=u(r)^2-2 int_0^r uu' and Young. Use actual
metric/weight comparison constants, choose epsilon small enough to
absorb negative Robin derivative charge, then a sufficiently large
positive bulk mass to dominate its negative value charge. Energy is
equivalent to H1 x L2. All remaining lower-order and boundary weight
work is bounded by that energy, so dE/dt<=K E and zero initial energy
gives A=0 by Gronwall. Now B_n=0 and B_{a,r}=j B_a; repeat the same
mixed D/R argument to get B=0. Reverse fold gives C=0. BR92 with
Ered=0 yields Ric=-4g; BR90/91 defect sources then vanish.
This is conditional uniqueness, not existence or acceptance for
original nonsymmetric BN data, not an independent Weyl proof.

**Exact general-background obstruction:** if reflected operators
Lp,Lm differ, Lbar=(Lp+Lm)/2, Delta=(Lp-Lm)/2, the equations become
  Lbar A+Delta B=0, Lbar B+Delta A=0.
Delta may be SECOND ORDER away from the seam, even though h matches.
A restricted constant face Gram G=[[a,b],[b,c]] in the scalar normal
subblock cancels D's free inward derivative only if b=c (flux has
(b-c)xy+2j c x²). Commutation with unequal diagonal principal speeds
forces b=0, contradicting positive c. This rules out that chosen
constant cross-face energy, NOT general microlocal symmetrizers.
Check actual BN/BQ reflection and gauge before attempting to instantiate
the cascade. Do not assume symmetry or switch to E.

**BR95 (W3):** With sigma=Re s>0, Z=max(|s|,|xi_i|), L=|ell|,
factor ell²=(s+i|xi|)(s-i|xi|) to obtain L²>=sigma Z. Independent
complex perturbations of the four z coordinates with |delta|<=sigma/64
give |delta ell²|<=8Z delta+4delta²<L²/4. Same holomorphic root chart:
|ell'/ell-1|<=1/4, |ell'|>=3L/4, |a'-a|<=|a|/3+1/48.
Since |1+a|>=max(1,|a|) originally, the perturbed Lorentz denominator
stays >=half that bound. |v|²<=1+|a|² at real frequency gives a
conservative 128Q row norm bound for perturbed boost and inverse;
rank-two lifts cost Q². Cauchy bounds fixed-metric frequency derivatives
by alpha! (64/sigma)^|alpha| times corresponding chart power.
This is a damping-scale bound, not conventional symbol order reduction
or control of variable metric derivatives. Indeed vary principal spatial
coefficient c², ell²=s²+c² k²; on s=sigma+i k, c=1,
  partial_c log ell=k²/(sigma²+2i sigma k).
For k>=sigma its squared modulus is between k²/(5sigma²) and
k²/(4sigma²): geometry derivative costs a full frequency power here.
No ordinary zero-order localization estimate follows from BR89.
Need genuine variable glancing patches or coupled defect energy.

## 11. BR104–107 — curved F wall-wave, foliation subsidiary and hidden trace

Reconciled actual BR26/100 material inverse, BR74/75/78 physical

### BR104: exact curved normal curvature and scale wave

On each material collar let psi=log lambda, n=e^-psi(∂y-beta.d),
K=1/2 Lie_n h, tau=tr_h K, Q=C_perp+H_perp=n psi-tau.
D is the intrinsic Lorentzian leaf connection, a=∇_n n=-D psi
(spacelike normal sign); K² below is signed tr(h^-1 K)^2, not
an assumed positive Lorentz norm. The exact identities are
 Ric_nn=-n tau-tr(K²)-Box_h psi-<D psi,D psi>_h,
 Box_g y=-e^-psi Q,
 Box_g psi=(n psi)^2-tr(K²)+n Q-Q n psi-Ric_nn.
The last follows from scalar Bochner on y: Hess y has ttangent
block e^-psi K, mixed -e^-psi D psi, nn=-e^-psi n psi.
With Ered=Ric+4g-S(C), Ric_nn=n C_perp+
<D psi,C_tan>+Ered_nn-4, hence
 Box_g psi=tau n psi-tr(K²)+n H_perp
            -<D psi,C_tan>+4-Ered_nn.
The actual material acceleration term is indispensable: n of the
normal component is NOT the Gaussian extension derivative of that
component unless normal acceleration vanishes. Direct independent
full 5x5 Christoffel, Ricci, scalar Hessian calculation at a
non-diagonal Lorentz point with variable shift, scale and rational
2-jets passes all three curvature/acceleration/Bochner identities.
No use of Ric=-4g before C=0.

### BR105: nonlinear wall-scale jump equation

On F wall, same h and geometric C, [K]=jh, tau+ + tau-=0,
tau±=±2j. Thus [tr(K²)]=2j tr M=0. The physical Gauss identity
[G_nn]=0 implies [Ric_nn]=tr_h[Ric_ab]. Since tangential
S(C)_ab=D_(a C_b)+K_ab C_perp, this equals
4j C_perp + tr_h[Ered_ab].
Taking the jump of BR104 gives the exact curved wall wave
 Box_h p + <D(psi+ + psi-),D p>_h
   = -[n tau]-4j C_perp-tr_h[Ered_ab], p=[psi].
This is valid for distinct reflected metrics and normal scale,
with a common wall h and fixed material foliation. The drift
is a genuine tangential first-order term; it does not vanish
by Israel. If reduced equations hold Ered=0; retain C until
BR98 has been proved on the same development. At normalized
flat principal linearization tau=-U, recovering BR102 including
the correct sign. A different normal-coordinate foliation gives
a different psi: this is a coordinate scale channel, not a new
physical radion or a gauge-invariant curvature degree.

### BR106: bulk mean-curvature subsidiary, no third metric jet on shell

For n^flat=e^psi dy, div n=tau, let B=Box_g psi-|dpsi|_g²
(signed contraction), V=∇_(grad psi) n. The product rule and
covector commutator give
 Box_g n^flat-Ric(n)^flat
   = B n^flat+2 V^flat-dQ+Q dpsi.
The divergence of Box_g n^flat-Ric(n)^flat equals Box_g tau
by the Hodge Laplacian/divergence commutation. Therefore
 Box_g tau=div_g(B n+2V-Q_gradient+Q grad psi),
 V=K^sharp(D psi)-n(psi) D psi (view tangent vector in g).
Expanded:
 Box_g tau=n B+tau B+2 div_g V-Box_g Q+
             <dQ,dpsi>+Q Box_g psi.
Independent exact differential check with curved Lorentz
h=e^(2f) eta4, lambda=e^psi, nonconstant f,psi in t,x,y and
two spectators passes. This test is not the general proof;
the general proof is the tensor product and Hodge identity above.

For a GIVEN regular reduced solution Ered=0, substitute BR104
into B. It becomes a first-jet expression if H(x,g) algebraic
and the material defining function fixed. Then n B and div V
use at most second metric jets. Reduce the apparent third jets
in Box Q by writing Q=C(n)+H_perp:
 Box Q=(Box C)(n)+2 contraction(∇C,∇n)+C(Box n)+Box H_perp.
BR92 gives Box C=-Ric(C) on the same reduced metric; Box n
uses the preceding product formula and at most second jets;
Box H_perp uses at most second metric jets for algebraic H.
Thus on shell the tau source can be expressed with at most
SECOND metric jets, not third; with nonzero residual retain
div TREred and n Ered_nn. For solution-dependent chart changes
or derivative-dependent H this order statement requires new
proof. This is a nonlinear identity for an actual regular
reduced solution, not an independent existence theorem for tau.
For homogeneous F tau±=±2j Dirichlet on the original wall.
Use a smooth prescribed collar lift of these two constants;
w=tau-lift has zero wall trace, source includes Box(lift).

### BR107: hidden normal trace and explicit reconstruction debt

For a GIVEN regular curved scalar Dirichlet wave Box w=F,

### BR109 — estimate the three operators jointly

Given zero initial/lifted data in the Laplace transformed flat
half-line scalar Dirichlet problem (∂r²-ell²)w=F on r>=0,
the normal derivative at zero is -integral exp(-ell r)F dr,
up to the chosen orientation. Its norm is exactly

L² norm, the functional norm is a^(-1/2). In the homogeneous
principal BR105 wall equation p has magnitude |jump n w|/L²,

The wave lift v has two-face L² norm |p|/(2L sqrt(a)).

the product mapping F to any second derivative of v
(one derivative of metric L, apart from fixed tensor/norm constants)

Exact root bounds: write ell=a+i b.
 (a²-gamma²)(a²+omega²)=a² |xi|², so a>=gamma.
 a² L²-gamma² |s|²=a⁴-gamma⁴ >=0.
 a² L²-gamma² |xi|²=a²(a²-gamma²)+gamma²(b²+gamma²)>=0.
Hence a L>=gamma m, L²>=gamma m, L²<=2m².
It follows that D²/(2 L³ a)<=gamma^-2.

half-line flat domain, including glancing, not just a ray.

multiplier bound holds if the input has the same weight.

source; nor replace the variable curved BR106 source by a free

H¹ lift thus loses a quarter tangential derivative in norm.

### BR120 — full nonlinear 15-row derivative jump inverse (continuous metric chart)

At a matched full metric g, timelike graph with defining covector F (F_y=1), lambda=(g^-1(F,F))^-1/2, n_flat=lambda F, E the four tangent columns, h=E^T g E. Differentiated continuity gives [∂_mu g]=F_mu J, J=[∂_y g]. The direct maps are

Let T be the ambient tangential extension of B (T=g E h^-1 B h^-1 E^T g). Then for arbitrary symmetric B and covector a the exact unique inverse is

Proof by tangent-normal decomposition: tangent block 2B, mixed block a_tan, normal block 2 tr_h B+2a_n in proper-normal derivatives. Direct readback pays all 10 Israel and 5 contracted rows. Rank 15 at any admissible continuous chart, no tangential characteristic inverse. Independent 15x15 exact Jacobian at nonorthogonal Lorentz point with shift, tilted graph, lambda=5/4 passes.

For pure tension B=j h and geometric gauge continuity, a=[H_cov] in the common chart:

             +6j sym2(delta n,n)+sym2(delta n,a)+sym2(n,delta a)].
Here sym2(u,v)=u⊗v+v⊗u. For graph motion delta F_a=-d_a f; material derivative jump is [delta g_y]+f[g_yy]. For fixed constant tension delta j=0; arbitrary j variation only tests source map, not vacuum pure-tension admissibility. delta a is actual material source variation (including evaluation/metric/source transport); it is not silently zero. Independent Sherman–Morrison rank-one metric path and nonzero tilt/source variations check exact derivative. The inverse concerns a common full g, not arbitrary unequal-lambda F background.

### BR122 — actual geometric factorization of the coordinate shear

For any smooth vector X and scalar v on a Lorentz metric,
 [Box_g,X]v=2(∇^mu X^nu) Hess_g(v)_mu,nu
             +(Box_g X+Ric^sharp(X))·dv.

 first scalar coefficients are g^ab ∂ab X^c-G^a∂a X^c+X^a∂a G^c,

For X=n, ∇n decomposes into K and normal acceleration. The tangential Hessian contribution is 2 K^ab Hess_g(v)_ab. ### BR128 — variable material projected energy, no normal derivative in tangential commutators

lower term L_g, and Cotton source F_g from BR91:
 e0 w = sum_{i=1..3} A_i e_i w + A_n n w + L_g w + F_g.
The connection here is the actual metric frame connection on
both bivector pairs, not the toy conjugation of BR40e. For the
geometric Weyl field the equation is obtained by selecting the
divergence and time-containing Bianchi rows and projecting into
the 35D algebraic Weyl carrier. Projection does not imply the
other unprojected Bianchi equations for an independent field.

Write n=lambda^-1(partial_y-beta^a partial_a), a=0..3. Multiply
by lambda to obtain
 C^a partial_a w - A_n partial_y w = lambda L_g w + lambda F_g,
 C^a=lambda(e0^a I-sum_i e_i^a A_i)+beta^a A_n.
Each G C^a is symmetric. The normal matrix G A_n is CONSTANT
in these coordinates, even for unequal lambda and arbitrary
one-sided beta. The raw Maxwell matrix for spatial vector v
acts between electric and magnetic slots by wedge v and its
Euclidean adjoint; norm <=|v|. Compression into the Weyl
subspace in the positive component Gram cannot enlarge that
bound. The analogous raw wall matrix acts on the time column
and spatial columns by v and its adjoint, norm <=|v|. Thus
timelike dt with positive e0(t) gives strictly positive G C^t
and H D^t on the wall, with quantitative margins
 lambda(e0(t)-sqrt(sum_{i=1..4} e_i(t)^2)) G,
 (e0(t)-sqrt(sum_{i=1..3} e_i(t)^2)) H respectively.
Need uniform lower and upper comparability on the chart.

For the wall write D^a=e0^a I-sum_i e_i^a B_i (B_i BR29),
 D^a partial_a m = L_h m-R mean(w)+f_wall.
Use common coordinate tangential measure dx^1dx^2dx^3 (or
a common smooth positive weight in both energies; include its
derivatives in lower terms). Define energy on a compact slab
 E=1/2 int_bulk w^T G C^t w + (-3j)/2 int_wall m^T H D^t m.
Here j=-6/5, -3j=18/5>0. For the active jump
 A_n([w]-j J m)=0, the signed bulk normal work is
 -mean(w)^T G A_n [w]=-3j mean(w)^T R^T H m;
 the wall exchange contributes +3j m^T H R mean(w).
Exact cancellation by BR27/29, also if only active jump is
imposed, because G A_n P0=0 and R P0=0.
No assumption of equal normal lapse or reflection symmetry.
Energy derivative consists of bounded coefficient-divergence,
connection work, bulk and wall source pairings and exterior
flux. On compact charts with uniform time coercivity and
bounded coefficients/first derivatives, homogeneous nonincoming
outer flux (or separately controlled forcing), Cauchy/Young and
Gronwall give
 E(t)<=exp(Kt)(E(0)+K int_0^t (||F_g||²+||f_wall||²)ds)
with K depending on actual chart bounds, not frequency.
This is an a priori estimate for sufficiently regular solutions
on a GIVEN metric. It does not establish their existence.
The wall shape L² is paid directly by this energy, without
first proving a separate trace estimate on each Weyl face.
Individual active or zero-speed Weyl traces are NOT bounded by
this L² energy alone.

For tangential spacetime multiindex alpha, differentiating the
scaled material equation gives no normal-principal commutator:
 [partial^alpha,P] w =
 sum_{0<nu<=alpha} binom(alpha,nu)
 (partial^nu C^a) partial_a partial^(alpha-nu)w
 minus the lower-coefficient commutators if included in P.
The analogous wall coefficient derivatives are tangential;
R,J,A_n and G,H constant in the common adapted frame. Thus
the differentiated active jump and exchange still cancel.
For integer s>=0, uniform bounded coefficient derivatives
through s+1 (or later proved tame product bounds), compatible
initial spacetime tangent jets through s, and differentiated
outer work hypotheses, summing all |alpha|<=s gives E_s'
<=K_s E_s+K_s source_s². There is no normal derivative
of the zero-speed sector in this commutator, nor derivative
loss relative to tangential curvature order on a fixed
background with those coefficient bounds. This is NOT a tame
quasilinear estimate in unknown metric regularity, a full H^s
normal estimate, nor a free exterior estimate. Partition of
unity on common tangential charts requires matched wall weights
and connection-compatible frames. Do not assume a global
orthonormal frame or global uniform time margin.

### BR129 — absorb actual Ricci defect into wall energy; conditional W4b uniqueness

In a matched F metric, D=Ric+4g, tr_h M=0, [K]=jh.
BR125 gives [w]=j J m_eff, with m_eff=m+delta, where delta
is the 9-coordinate encoding of tf_h[D_tan]/(3j).
This is an exact source shift, not an assumption of vacuum.
Mean Codazzi in our convention is
 D^a M_ab=mean D_nb,
 D_0 M_ia = D_i M_0a - mean Riem_{n a 0 i}.
With frame derivatives and the intrinsic connection separated,
the raw wall source from Ricci defect in column i=0 is

 -(mean D_n0 h_ai - mean D_ni h_a0)/3
since (g wedge Sch)_{n a 0 i}=Sch_n0 h_ai-Sch_ni h_a0,

9-coordinate wall carrier. This source is not generically zero.
Thus for m_eff the wall equation has the SAME principal/exchange
matrices, with forcing
 f_eff=f_Codazzi+e0 delta-sum_i B_i e_i delta-L_h delta.
Frame dependence of the tracefree operation is included by
using the same wall orthonormal frame; its derivatives are
part of these actual tangential derivatives. No arbitrary
boundary flux pairing with an uncontrolled mean w trace
remains from the algebraic Ricci jump; it is paid in wall
forcing. Bulk forcing remains the actual Cotton source F_g.
Regularity requirement: tangential derivatives of f_eff through
order s involve order s+1 of the wall Ricci-defect trace; bulk
F_g has one derivative of D. Not a free metric energy estimate.

Conditional W4b theorem: on the SAME given sufficiently regular
matched F metric, with D from that metric, same material frame,
connection, actual Cotton/Codazzi/Schouten sources, same
geometric initial (w,m_eff), and same exterior conditions,
any sufficiently regular projected solution with active jump
A_n([w]-j J m_eff)=0 equals the actual geometric Weyl and
effective mean shape. Proof: the geometric pair satisfies the
projected bulk equations by differential Bianchi and projected
wall equations by Gauss/Codazzi, and the full jump by BR125.
Subtract, apply BR128 homogeneous energy with zero initial
data and zero/nonincoming exterior difference work, conclude
w_diff=m_eff_diff=0. Then the full zero-speed jump and all

rather than independently assuming an arbitrary projected

conditional same-metric preservation implication on a regular
compact chart. Global weighted uniqueness needs the exterior
and localization hypotheses. It does NOT construct a metric,
an independent projected solution, or solve the Cartan

BR53/91 remain relevant to construction, not overwritten.

Given g, a possible projected weak generation route on a

### BR130 — actual lapse acceleration in normal curvature transport, weighted inverse

BR127 Gaussian inverse is not directly the material identity.
On arbitrary regular timelike leaves write A=h^-1 K,
a=nabla_n n=-D psi, psi=log lambda, and use the projected
normal derivative D_n on mixed tangential tensors:
 R_e=(R_{n a n b})^sharp
   =-D_n A-A²+(D a)^sharp-a^sharp tensor a_flat
   =-D_n A-A²-lambda^-1(Hess_h lambda)^sharp.
The second identity uses a=-D log lambda and the Hessian
chain rule. Trace gives exactly BR104 Ric_nn.
Independent rational 5D nonumbilic variable lapse/metric
two-jet oracle with non-diagonal h, zero shift, checks all

The curvature sign and acceleration term also follow from

Linearizing in a fixed normal-parallel comparison frame yields
 D_n B+A B+B A=-E_eff, B=delta A,

       +(delta D_n)A.
All frame, metric and normal variations in the last terms
are retained; this is not closure of the lapse-Hessian debt.

Let r be proper normal along a regular segment, U'=U A,
V'=A V, U(0)=V(0)=I, Z=U B V. Then Z'=-F,
F=U E_eff V. For any positive integrable weight omega,
let Qomega=int omega, and momega=Qomega^-1 int omega Z
(the TRANSPORTED moment). Exact weighted primitive inverse:
 Z(r)=momega+Qomega^-1 int_0^d omega(x) int_0^x F
                        -int_0^r F,
 B=U^-1 Z V^-1.
For |A|<=a0 on length d and weight 0<omega_min<=omega<=omega_max,
using ||Z||<=|momega|+2 int |F| gives for positive
submultiplicative component norms
 ||B||_{L2(omega)} <= exp(2a0 d) sqrt(Qomega)|momega|
      +2 exp(4a0 d) sqrt(Qomega int omega^-1)
                           ||E_eff||_{L2(omega)}.
Cauchy-Schwarz supplies the last factor. For scalar A=k I,
b(0)=1, U=V=b I and omega=1, this recovers BR57's
b² shear moment. General transported moment must be attached
to original BR31/BS11 graph/holonomy, not freely set to zero.
Alternatively BR125 supplies B(0) if actual metric/wall
regularity and Ricci trace are paid. Uniform normal segment
length, coefficient and weight distortion across the actual
Hopf foliation, global coordinate coverage and focal/holonomy
compatibility remain W5.

Exterior: reopen BR31/BS12 actual static reference weights
N³ dvol ~ R^4 dR, outer curvature work ~ R^6 W_even W_odd.
For even W=O(R^-p) and odd W=O(R^-(p+1)), p>=3,
energy tail integrable and flux tends zero (p=3 gives

### BR133 — restore all 12 wall evolution rows and pay tangential skew using spatial dH

### BR134 — covariant exterior sources on unknown geometry

Let Omega be an actual connection on an independent
invertible coframe theta, R an algebraic Riemann
endomorphism-valued two-form, F_Omega=dOmega+Omega²,

covariant differentiation squares to the graded
commutator with F_Omega. Hence exactly

Because [R,R]=0 and [R,*R]=0 (the latter pointwise
by symmetry of the form inner product and antisymmetry
of matrix commutators), the right sides are algebraic
in the curvature defect, not derivatives of E or R.
No metric derivative is omitted by using the exterior
identity with the actual varying Hodge: it is included
in D. Independent polynomial noncommuting 2x2 matrices
on all five coordinate dimensions verify both equations,
the Hodge contraction and nonzero source control.

of BR132's spatial dC_B compatibility, and temporal
evolution of the spatial exterior constraints with
the exterior electric/magnetic residuals.
They do NOT by themselves imply the full projected
subsidiary system has only homogeneous lower terms.
The original BR29 projected equations use full covariant
frame derivatives. Their conversion to d_Omega forms
includes torsion times R, and frame/Hodge coefficients;
in a reduced nonvacuum metric retain Ricci derivatives
in the divergence source. Need the actual variable
frame residual graph and differentiated projection or
choose an exterior constraint-added evolution (which
agrees when torsion=0) and prove the wall attachment.
No use of vacuum Bianchi on an unconstrained unknown
connection.

contribution, with the overall j removed, is
 Z_M=(-D_n^red X A_n^+ C_M
          +sum_i D_i^red L_{M,i} X) J m.
Here D_n^red selects the original independent constraint
rows; the normal derivative is the covariant normal
derivative, or a frame parallel in a Gaussian collar.
This expression follows from the difference of the bulk
equations with full jump jJm: the connection difference
contributes j C_h mean(w)+j C_M Jm. Solve only the active
normal derivative with A_n^+ and retain the free P0 part.
For every symbolic tracefree M, the exact actual BR29 matrices give
 P0 C_M Jm=0,
 P_c Z_M=0,
 S_c Z_M=0,
 Mag (sum_i M_i L_{M,i}-L_{M,0}) X Jm=0.
Six entries of Z_M in the original selected 35-coordinate
basis are nonzero polynomials. Thus one MUST NOT set Z_M=0
or discard the zero-speed component of the constraint graph.
It is invisible to this active projection and this selected
normal-work form, not the full residual. This extends BR133's
reference extrinsic mean contribution to arbitrary nonzero
mean M, including the actual AN/BN mean rather than assuming
reflection. Does not prove a general variable seam identity:
common intrinsic connection, full jump, compatible derivatives,
actual normal frame transport, torsion and Ricci corrections
remain hypotheses/source terms. Intrinsic time-frame connection
terms and coordinate-dependent graph/trace effects must not
be silently set to zero. The symbolic check is the full
quadratic polynomial in all nine original m coordinates.

### BR137 — local compatible subsidiary current and tilted time mass

BR133 had an integrated wall correction using spatial dH.
Write wall Hodge coordinates u=(e,X), tr X=0, and embedding
T from 11 coordinates to the full 12 cyclic rows. Let G
be the old positive mass with quadratic form
 (3/2)|e|²+3||X_s||²+6||X_a||².
The actual full multiplier in these coordinates is
 W=-T(T^T T)^-1 G:
it has T^T W=-G and is orthogonal to the cyclic annihilator,
so agrees with the unique BR133 multiplier after coordinate
change. Use the same raw antisymmetric wall projection RW,
full curl Dh_i and F_i=-W^T Dh_i RW.
For Emap u=e and row_i u=(X_{i,k})_k, exact skew:
 F_i-F_i^T=-(3/2)(Emap^T row_i-row_i^T Emap).
Consequently, in a constant frame,
 u^T F_i partial_i u =
 (1/2) partial_i(u^T sym F_i u)
 +(3/4) partial_i(e·row_i u) -(3/2)e·partial_i row_i u.
Sum over i. The spatial Bianchi compatibility and full wall
equation give div_T X=2 e_t at reference level. Hence with
 G_f=G+3 Emap^T Emap,
 S_i=sym F_i+(3/4)(Emap^T row_i+row_i^T Emap),
the corrected local current has energy u^T G_f u/2 and
spatial flux u^T S_i u/2 (balance written with the spatial
divergence on the RHS), times the common positive -j.
It retains BR133's normal exchange with the extended bulk.

Actual 11x11 positive mass and each G_f^-1 S_i have exact
eigenvalues -1/2 (2), -1/3 (1), 0 (5), 1/3 (1), 1/2 (2).
For the SO(3) action e->Oe, X->OXO^T, all three
infinitesimal generators L satisfy
 L^T G_f+G_f L=0,
 L^T S_i+S_i L+sum_k Ogen_{ki} S_k=0.
This proves the same scaled spectrum for EVERY spatial
direction v: eigenvalues multiplied by |v|. Thus in a
common wall frame, on a coordinate time slice with future
timelike dt, the corrected energy time mass
 e0(t) G_f-sum_i e_i(t) S_i
has lower bound (e0(t)-|e_sp(t)|/2) G_f.
This is a compatible current, not a free-jet symmetric
evolution for all 11 variables; use the spatial Bianchi
relation for the skew conversion. The wall characteristic
speeds here are those of the corrected flux relative to its
mass, NOT a claim that the physical propagation speeds of
all subsidiary unknowns equal these eigenvalues.

For variable frame vector fields, the local divergence
conversion contributes bounded frame divergence and intrinsic
connection work if those coefficients are bounded. If
 e0 e=(div_T X-div X)/2+f_e, div X=2 f_e+b
where the symbols denote frame component derivatives and
f_e,b include all actual lower terms, then
 div_T X=2 e0 e+b.
Residual work after moving the mass term is exactly
 -(3/2)e·b, times -j, plus the divergence terms above.
No bulk trace source is discarded; b measures the real
covariant compatibility defect. BR135 supplies part of b
through the tangential Cartan defect, not a priori zero.
This permits local coordinate patches and common positive
weights, unlike an argument that assumes a flat tangential
torus. Weight derivatives contribute lower work and require
actual uniform bounds. No claim of global AdS reflecting
domain or exterior weighted coercivity.

For the extended bulk residual variables in BR29 the
matrices U_i are constant in the adapted frame. Their
uncompressed symbol [-M(v),-D(v)^T;-D(v),0] has norm <=|v|
by BR29 M(v)^2+D(v)^T D(v)=|v|²I, D(v)M(v)=0
and its squared block diagonal. Compression by diag(Q,I)
does not increase norm. Multiplying by the material lambda
as BR128 gives constant normal principal matrix U_n,
symmetric tangential matrices and positive time mass for
timelike dt. This is the extended system, not the naive
35-coordinate restricted symmetrizer refused in BR132.
This geometric principal observation assumes that actual
curved lower/source and graph conversion have been supplied.

### BR138 — precise exterior/frame conversion with torsion retained

For any endomorphism-valued p-form F in a coframe theta
with compatible connection Omega, let nabla act on both
the form slots and the endomorphism slots using the induced
connection (not assumed torsion-free), T^a its Cartan torsion.
With unnormalized alternating derivative convention,
 D_Omega F = Alt(nabla F)+sum_a T^a wedge i_{e_a} F.
This follows from differentiating the coframe factors in
F and adding/subtracting the connection on their slots.
The exact correction for p=2 or 3 is algebraic in T and F.
The local rational point-jet oracle independently uses
arbitrary coframe first derivatives at theta=I, independent
Lorentz connection coefficients and arbitrary independent
endomorphism-valued coefficient first jets, computes the
coordinate exterior derivative and the alternating frame
derivative, and verifies every degree-3 and degree-4 component.
The correction is nonzero in both tests; torsion-free
conversion cannot be applied off shell.
In particular for F=R and F=*R,
 the exterior Bianchi residual = frame residual + T*R
 (or T*(*R)). For metric-compatible Omega the internal
Hodge is parallel, but the coframe contribution remains.
BR134's d_Omega C=[E,R], d_Omega D=[E,*R] are exact without
torsion-free assumptions. If one directly evolves the
frame residual using these exterior relations, derivatives
of the correction T*R occur; use exterior residual variables
or pay the differentiated torsion equation. The projected
frame evolution has to be adjusted consistently, including
its wall sources, before claiming a homogeneous first-order
subsidiary with no derivative loss. The identity alone does
not make the exterior residual graph equal to the BR29
frame graph on unknown torsionful geometry.

### BR139 — exact Cartan pullback trace budget and remaining derivative payment

In a regular material coordinate collar y with tangential
spatial x^i and temporal gauge E_ti=0, let E=F_Omega-R,
C=D_Omega R. From D_Omega E=-C,
 partial_y E_ij = -C_yij-partial_i E_jy+partial_j E_iy
 -[Omega_y,E_ij]-[Omega_i,E_jy]+[Omega_j,E_iy].
Here i,j are tangential spatial coordinate indices and
C_yij=C_ijy. Exact noncommuting polynomial connection and
defect forms independently verify every such component.
With E_ti=0, the full tangential pullback consists of these
spatial entries. Under bounded connection and chart
comparability the normal derivative of E_parallel is paid
by C, E and ONE tangential derivative of E_mixed. Similarly
D_Omega T=E wedge theta on algebraic R wedge theta=0 gives
the spatial torsion pullback normal derivative in terms of
E,T and a tangential derivative of T_mixed.
This does not require a second normal derivative.

For any component f on a finite one-sided coordinate
collar [0,d] the exact scalar trace identity is
 |f(0)|²=d^-1 int |f|²-2 int (1-y/d) f f_y.
Thus for vector/matrix fields in positive component norms,
after tangential integration,
 ||f|wall||² <= d^-1||f||²+2||f||||f_y||
 <= (d^-1+epsilon^-1)||f||²+epsilon||f_y||².
With smooth common positive measures, retain their derivative
terms or use equivalent coordinate norms. BR135 tangential
Cartan trace work therefore has an explicit conditional
bound in terms of bulk E,C and one extra mixed tangential
jet of E, plus analogous torsion terms if needed. At a
higher tangential derivative order, commutators use actual
connection and coefficient regularity. This does NOT close
a same-order combined energy: the temporal Cartan defect
transport E_t=-C_t has no tangential smoothing, so that
extra derivative cannot be assumed free. Explicit boundary
layer f_k=exp(-ky) has wall value 1 and L² norm squared
1/(2k); even zero-speed temporal transport cannot furnish
an L²-only boundary trace inequality. This is a trace
counterexample, not a full constrained solution counterexample.
Need compatible surface evolution or a source-factorized
mixed-order estimate retaining the actual spatial exterior
constraints, rather than adding arbitrary trace terms to
Gronwall. The covariant wall b residual of BR137 is the
specific consumer; identify the remaining source directions.

## 20. BR140–142 — surface Cartan BIDI, actual anisotropic source and time evolution

Consume BR135/137/139, not another generic Gauss theory.
In an actual matched common wall coframe theta with common intrinsic
connection omega, k^±=M^sharp(theta)±j theta/2 and k_flat=k^T h.
For a metric-compatible ambient block connection
 Omega=[omega,k; -k_flat,0],
 the tangential curvature pullback is F_omega-k wedge k_flat,
 and the normal-tangential row is -D_omega k_flat.
These are direct Cartan block formulas.

BR140: at constant j the tangential jump is exactly
 -j(k_M wedge theta_flat+theta wedge k_M_flat).
If target R has the same full Gauss jump (vacuum BR125), then
 [E_parallel]=0 for E=F_Omega-R.
The mixed target R jump is zero, so [E_nt]=-j T_wall_flat with
 T_wall=D_omega theta. Intrinsic torsion has NOT disappeared
 off shell; common intrinsic omega and coframe, matching k and
 target jumps are hypotheses, not consequences of arbitrary Cartan
 initial data. BR129 Ricci-defect corrections must be retained for
 reduced nonvacuum target. The mean source of BR135 becomes
 mean(Omega_nt wedge E_parallel)=-k_M_flat wedge E_parallel
 under [E_parallel]=0; the j terms cancel, not the mean term.

BR141: in the restricted algebraic sector where the tangential
 curvature defect has Riemann pair symmetry and vanishing time-form
 slots, all remaining indices are spatial. Let its double spatial
 Hodge dual be the symmetric 3x3 matrix S, so
 E_ijba=epsilon_ijl epsilon_bam S_lm.
 The spatial triple of -k_M_flat wedge E_parallel is exactly
 the axial vector of [M_sp,S]=M_sp S-S M_sp, using the stated
 orientation (K_12,K_20,K_01 with indices 0,1,2).
 Thus its nonzero shear work lies in three off-diagonal
 commutator directions for a simple-spectrum spatial mean.
 The rank-three example M_sp=diag(1,2,4) with S01 nonzero
 is compatible with tracefree full mean by setting M00=7.
 Commuting S and M_sp gives zero, pure trace M_sp gives zero.
 Neither condition follows from the full nonzero Hopf source.
 Generic off-shell E need NOT have Riemann pair symmetry:
 torsion and algebraic Cartan defects must be retained separately.
 This is a precise negative control to a scalar cancellation,
 not a counterexample to the full constrained system.

BR142: with coordinate time electric defect E_ti=0 (BR131),
 spatial wall two-form defect obeys
 partial_t E_ij+[Omega_t,E_ij]=-C_tij, C=D_Omega R.
 In an adapted wall frame write E_ij=[B,q;e,0],
 Omega_t=[omega_t,k_t;kn_t,0], kn_t=-k_t^T h.
 The actual surface block equations are
 partial_t B+[omega_t,B]=-C_tij,parallel-k_t e+q kn_t,
 partial_t e-e omega_t=-C_tij,nt-kn_t B.
 They are an ODE along coordinate time on the wall, with bounded
 coefficient homogeneous exchange in positive component norms.
 If the actual C_tij trace is controlled in L²_t L²_wall,
 then a Gronwall surface norm estimate follows with compatible
 initial surface data and common time chart. This does not require
 the adapted frame to be the temporal connection gauge.
 It also does NOT manufacture that Bianchi trace estimate
 from bulk L²: BR139 boundary layer refusal still applies.
 The next actual estimate is a joint active-flux/compatible
 surface evolution for the C_tij trace and the BR141 commutator,
 including torsion, or a justified mixed-order trace bound.
 W5 exterior and W6 generation remain; no PDE promotion.

### 21.2 BR143 — reverse-solve the exact electric trace quotient

Use the BR29/132 embedding X (100 by 35), orthogonal complement Q,
actual Maxwell matrices M_i, spatial residual maps D_i, and same
selected independent coordinates c. r=Lc, C_full=E_c c. Normal work
on this graph is represented by
 S_c=-L^T M_n L-L^T D_n^T E_c-E_c^T D_n L.
BR143 independently reconstructs the original order, constraints and
factors and checks L Dr=Rj, E_c Dr=Dj. In the reference flat-principal
frame graph the magnetic entries of r (up to the residual sign) give
C_{0ij,ab} for tangential spatial pairs i<j in 1..3 and second bivector
ab in 0..4. Let T be these 30 rows of L and T_sp the nine rows with
second pair also spatial in 1..3. The latter is exactly the restricted
spatial electric source for the BR141 double-dual curvature block.
Exact ranks:
 rank S_c=22, dim ker S_c=13,
 rank T=23, rank T restricted to ker S_c=4,
 rank T_sp=3, T_sp restricted to ker S_c=0.
If Z is a basis of ker S_c and J spans image(T Z), take
 P_perp=I-J(J^T J)^-1 J^T.
The exact solve U S_c=P_perp T yields the BIDI split of the trace
into its rank19 flux-factorable component and rank4 blind component.
The restricted spatial trace is wholly flux-factorable:
 T_sp=U_sp S_c.
The initial attempted negative assertion that T_sp saw the flux
kernel failed; the exact rank computation reverses that hypothesis.
This is a factorization through the NORMAL WORK MATRIX, not through
the scalar signed quadratic work c^T S_c c. An indefinite energy
flux does not bound ||S_c c||, and cancellation of net wall work
does not give the individual trace. The four remaining directions
are an algebraic kernel of the selected map, not independently
constructed PDE solutions. Need actual active characteristic/trace
estimate, compatible boundary evolution for blind directions,
and BR138 torsion/frame corrections; do not claim W2/W4b closed.
At arbitrary curved independent connection, E need not have the
restricted algebraic curvature symmetries of BR141, so the full
T trace and off-shell corrections remain in the budget.

### 21.3 BR144 — actual G3 central tidal tensor, not a new gravitational ansatz

Consume P0T1/V4 original h=psi²(delta+(dw+eps A0)²) with
time-symmetric K_init=0, original positive radial psi,
-Delta psi=(eps²/12)|B0|² psi. At x=0 put p=psi(0)>0.
Since |B0(0)|²=64, radial smoothness gives psi_i(0)=0 and
psi_ij(0)=-(16/9) eps² p delta_ij.
Using the exact original signed horizontal frame
 e_i=partial_i-eps A_i partial_w, U=partial_w,
and dividing each by p, the central physical orthonormal
spatial Ricci tensor is independently computed from the
coordinate metric and all first/second jets:
 T5=p^-2 [
   -208 eps²/9, 0, 0, 0;
   0, -208 eps²/9, 0, 0;
   0, 0, 80 eps²/9, 16 eps;
   0, 0, 16 eps, 112 eps²/3 ].
Here the sign convention is R_{i0j0}=Ric(h)_ij on a
time-symmetric vacuum slice, g_tt=-1. This follows from
the vacuum spatial Ricci contraction and Gauss, or
h_tt=-2 Ric(h) in proper normal time. T5 is the actual
electric curvature/tidal tensor at the initial event of
the admitted local G3 development, not a Weyl model or
an external source. Relative geodesic acceleration is
-T5 applied to the separation vector. The trace is zero,
as required by the actual Hamiltonian constraint.
The longitudinal/fibre subdeterminant before scaling
is (8960/27) eps^4-256 eps². It is negative when
0<eps²<27/35, in particular original eps=1/4.
Two transverse electric eigenvalues are negative and
the longitudinal/fibre block has opposite signs; no
isotropic attractive/repulsive interpretation follows.
Physical relative accelerations are opposite in sign
to these tidal eigenvalues. The mixed component 16 eps/p²
is linear in the signed field amplitude, while all the
diagonal entries are quadratic. At eps=0 the flat
control has zero tensor. The scalar solution depends
only on eps², and for eps=1/4 the previous contraction
bound is 1<=p<=18/17. These are dimensionless source
units and an initial local observable, not calibrated
4D detector output, antigravity or long-time stability.
The check uses independent coordinate Christoffel/Ricci
first/second jets; verifies the Taylor jet of the original
rational A0, not merely the target polynomial. The
analytic local-development and radial constraint hypotheses
are inherited from P0T/U, not re-proved by the checker.

### 21.4 BR145 — concrete TOE non-descent and signed repair

At fixed |eps|>0 and fixed oriented fibre frame, compare
the original two states eps and -eps. Their scalar psi
is the same by the P0T radial uniqueness argument.
Their entire horizontal 3x3 tidal block, vertical
diagonal and characteristic polynomial are equal.
T5(-eps)=R T5(eps) R, R=diag(1,1,1,-1).
The oriented mixed probe differs by 32 eps/p²:
 eps=p² T5_{z5}/16.
Thus the coarse observation of diagonal blocks and
spectrum is not sufficient for this signed consumer.
One normalized mixed probe recovers the signed amplitude
on this two-point fibre; no claim of arbitrary metric,
full state or dynamical history reconstruction.
The fibre reflection is a 5D isometry relating the
unoriented data, not a measurable absolute sign without
a marked fibre orientation. The result is a non-descent
of the source-indexed observer, not physical inequivalence
modulo all diffeomorphisms. It attaches to the actual
ConsumerNonDescentWitness and factorization blocker in
the existing TOE adapter, with supplied distinctness for

### BR146 — the four apparent blind channels are normal-active

Use BR143's actual graph B=[L;E_c], u=Bc. On the extended residual

 U_n=[-Q M_n Q, -Q D_n^T; -D_n Q,0].
The old normal-work matrix is S_c=B^T U_n B. Its rank22

Define A=U_n B (150x35). Exact:
 rank A=26, rank S_c=22,
 rank A|ker S_c=4, dim ker A=9,
 rank [A;T]=26, T|ker A=0.
The old rank-four full electric trace kernel image equals the
rank-four normal action image on ker S_c in the sense that the
combined restricted map has the same rank four (both have the same
kernel there). In particular the whole 30-row electric trace T
of rank23 factors through A. Select 26 independent rows Ar
of A, solve the rational V Ar=T. This is an actual explicit
matrix identity, not a scalar flux inequality. Also
 A^T A=B^T U_n² B is positive semidefinite, exact kernel9.
Thus no extra wall degrees of freedom are needed merely to observe
these four directions in the reference principal graph. The rank9
here refers to subsidiary normal action, not the nine Weyl zero
modes; do not identify them without a map.

An analytic trace consequence on a GIVEN regular material chart:

 C^a partial_a u-U_n partial_y u=f
where U_n is constant, C^a bounded and f includes actual lower work.
Then v=U_n u satisfies
 partial_y v=C^a partial_a u-f.
BR139's one-sided collar inequality gives (coordinate measures)
 ||T c|wall||² <= ||V||² [ d^-1 ||v||²+
      2 ||v|| (sum_a ||C^a||_infty ||partial_a u||+||f||) ],
using independent-row selection of v and exact graph u=Bc.
Same estimate after integrating in physical time, or by Cauchy
in spacetime. In constant reference time mass one can alternatively
multiply the equation by chi(y) U_n u, chi=1 at wall, zero
at outer collar, to get positive squared normal-symbol trace:
 for u_t=sum_i U_i partial_i u+U_n partial_y u+F,
 (1/2) int_wall ||U_n u||² =
 -(d/dt)(1/2 int chi u^T U_n u)

 + int chi (U_n u)^T(sum_i U_i partial_i u+F).

indefinite scalar work. On integration in t its endpoints cost
ordinary L² energy; tangential bulk work costs one spatial jet.

cost a time jet; use the direct normal derivative estimate above.

### BR152 — actual original G4 York source in radial-angular geometry

Consume the original P0H Fourier Y, with a=kappa s alpha
(s is the alignment sign), Bhat=4pi² exp(-q) q
 (z_hat-n n_z+i n cross z_hat). Define the real vector
potential V=(-Delta)^-1 B. Separate poloidal and toroidal:
 V=curl curl(z_hat F(r))+curl(z_hat G(r)),
 -Delta F=2/(1+r²), -Delta G=4/(1+r²)².
These radial source functions follow directly from the
inverse Fourier sine transforms. Regular derivatives:
 F'=-2(r-atan r)/r²,
 G'=-2(atan r-r/(1+r²))/r².
Only derivatives of F,G occur; additive constants are irrelevant.
Write V=v(r) z_hat+b(r) z x+c(r) z_hat cross x where
 v=-F''-F'/r, b=(F''-F'/r)/r², c=-G'/r.
Then original Y=-a (grad V+grad V^T), no arbitrary TT
addition. Set n=x/r, mu=n·z_hat, h=z_hat cross n,
 A=v'+br, B=2b' r², C=2br, D=c'r.
The literal tensor is
 Y=-a [ A(n z_hat^T+z_hat n^T)+B mu nn^T+C mu I
          +D(n h^T+h n^T)].
Trace 2A+B+3C=0. The two polar/toroidal parts are Frobenius
orthogonal, so
 |Y|²=a²[U(r)+V2(r)mu²],
 U=2(A²+D²),
 V2=2A²+B²+3C²+4AB+4AC+2BC-2D².
This is the actual source angular dependence, not an assumed
radial Y². The central limits A/r=-12/5, B/r=0,
 C/r=8/5, D/r=0 give Y(0)=0 and
 partial_l Y_ij(0)=a [
  (12/5)(delta_li z_j+delta_lj z_i)-(8/5)delta_ij z_l].
Independent angular Fourier second/fourth moments recover
all 27 derivatives. The divergence equals 8a z_hat, exactly
the original momentum source at centre. The axial and central
inversion symmetries of |Y|² and |B|² transfer to the unique
original positive contraction solution psi, hence grad psi(0)=0
and Hess log psi(0)=diag(Hx,Hx,Hz). Unlike G3, psi need not
be radial. At centre p=psi(0), the actual conformal equation
sets
 2 Hx+Hz=-16 kappa eps² p^-4,

Let f=2pi G_N eps² |B|² psi^-3+|Y|² psi^-7/8.
Newton Hessian difference has the exact convergent quadrature
 Hz-Hx=(3/(2p)) int_0^infty dr/r int_-1^1
       [mu²-(1-mu²)/2] f(r,mu) dmu.
The constant isotropic Hessian contact term cancels in the
difference. Smooth axial/even f gives f(r,mu)-f(0)=O(r²)
at the origin; far decay follows from original fields and
bounded psi (or explicit V radial formulas). This is a
two-dimensional quadrature of the SAME nonlinear solution,
not a replacement by radial averaging or a claimed evaluated
number. Actual central electric tensor is axisymmetric, with
trace from BR148 and difference
 T_zz-T_xx=-2(Hz-Hx)/p^4-kappa(S_parallel-S_perp).
Thus the full central diagonal matrix is determined by the
single explicit quadrupole integral plus the already-paid
trace and primitive readouts. The exact remaining mathematical
and numerical cut is evaluating/bounding this quadrupole on
the P0H contraction solution, not deriving a new York
tensor or assuming the stress alone controls the tide.

and weighted continuation not performed.

The cube of the lift has linear phase character
 chi(x)=f(x)+f(Ax)+f(A²x); chi is A-invariant.
Rephase f3=f+chi. Because 3 is odd, f3's orbit sum is zero.
Exact all-index polar/diagonal matrix identities plus 4672
basis/random controls verify the cocycle and cube. This
constructs a genuine order-three automorphism of the chosen
extraspecial plus-type extension, not a canonical FLM lift.

In P0AO's real irreducible 4096-dimensional Weyl representation,
the automorphism fixes the central sign. By real irreducibility
and uniqueness of the central-minus representation an orthogonal
intertwiner U_A exists; its cube is scalar ±I by the scalar
commutant, normalize by a real sign to make U_A³=I.
This is an analytical finite representation argument, not
an executed 4096x4096 matrix. Conjugation on End has the
Weyl basis indexed by all mod2 x, with phase f3(x).
At every A-fixed x the cube condition gives f3(x)=0.
Consequently tr Ad(U_A)=2^8=256=(tr U_A)^2.
Since U_A is real with order3, trace is integer and
tr U_A≡4096 mod3, so the sign is +16, not -16.
Ground complex eigenmultiplicities are (1376,1360,1360).
For the selected rank24 oscillator action P its eigen-
multiplicities are (8,8,8). On the product 98304 ground/
oscillator carrier the multiplicities are therefore
(32768,32768,32768), character zero.

(tr P²+(tr P)^2)/2=0. The selected weight-two direct-sum
character is 0, not the actual V2 3B character 54.

### BR154 — physical shell characters and periodic-table distinction

At principal n the source spin-orbital set is (l,m,s),
0<=l<n, -l<=m<=l, s=±1 (twice spin). Its dimension is
2 n². The orbital-only C3 rotates with omega^m; the spin
spectator supplies a factor two. For n=2 the multiplicities
(4,2,2) give trace2; n=3 gives (6,6,6) and trace0.
In general each l contributes orbital trace 1,0,-1 repeating
with l mod3; the shell sum is 1 for n mod3!=0, zero otherwise.
Physical spin rotation by 2pi/3 has order six, not three.
For the separately chosen order-three lift (minus the physical
spin rotation), its two spin eigenvalues are omega and omega²;
the product orbital/spin multiplicities for n=2 are (2,3,3),
trace -1. This is not the literal physical spin rotation.
The checker enumerates n=1..9 and source period partitions
2,8,8,18,18,32,32. Filled shell determinant phases are neutral.
A principal n=3 shell of 18 states is not periodic-table period
3 (3s+3p, eight states). Retain principal, orbital, spin, charge
and filling order. No ground-state exception or nuclear stability
derivation. The 8 in n=2 is 2+6, not a claim that atomic orbitals
are the eight geometric points or E8 roots.

Continuum domain argument for the terminal owner's firstLiveLeaf:
for fixed nucleus at origin and N electrons in R³, the orbital
rotation acts by (U_R f)(x_1,...)=f(R^-1 x_1,...); global spin
rotation acts by the tensor product of its SU2 matrices. They
are unitary on the antisymmetric Hilbert space since they commute
with particle permutation. On the Sobolev kinetic domain H² the
chain rule and orthogonal R give invariant domain and Laplacian
covariance; central |x_i|^-1 and pair |x_i-x_j|^-1 multiplication
are invariant and infinitesimally kinetic-bounded by the standard
Hardy/relative-bound estimates. Equivalently the Coulomb quadratic
form on H¹ is invariant, so the associated self-adjoint Hamiltonian
intertwines by uniqueness of the form operator. A finite CI
projection onto exterior powers of complete rotational subshells
commutes with U_R; its compressed Hamiltonian therefore intertwines.
This is a written classical domain argument for the fixed-nucleus
nonrelativistic Coulomb source, not a kernel proof, a relativistic
domain proof or a new general atomic theory. Incomplete subshells,
external fields and configuration-dependent truncations need their
own invariant-domain check.

### BR159 — same-phase matter/frame discriminator on G3

Do not select a physical detector metric by the sign of its
optical curvature. Use #1241's independent bulk Maxwell field
and a periodic zero-mode Dirac carrier, with supplied 5D mass m
and reference coordinate/circle normalization L. Let f=phi/phi_ref
be dimensionless and g_E=f g_J (constant reference normalization
absorbed). In a locally constant-radius leading zero-mode
approximation, the 5D measure reduces to f sqrt(-g_J), Maxwell
kinetic factor to f, and the canonical Jordan Dirac normalization

### BR169 — actual differential positive-energy Pauli source

With the BR166 Clifford conventions and mass grading
beta=-i Gamma^0=diag(I2,-I2), the spatial odd Dirac
operator has upper-right block
 D_+=-i sigma dot p-sigma dot b(x),
and lower-left adjoint D_- = i sigma dot p-sigma dot b(x).
Here p=-i grad and b is the geometric compact Pauli
coefficient vector, not the independent physical Maxwell
magnetic field. Squaring the actual odd operator gives
 D_+ D_- = p²+|b|²+div b
               +sigma dot(2 b cross p+i curl b).
The independent matrix/first-jet check verifies all
momentum and derivative coefficients, not just the
constant-field symbol. The spin-momentum term is formally
Hermitian on smooth compactly supported spinors; its
matrix elements between real scalar orbitals f,g are
 -i sigma dot int b cross(f grad g-g grad f) d³x.
This follows by integration by parts in the curl term,
with vanishing boundary terms. In particular a single
real spatial orbital has no diagonal linear contribution
from this term.

The leading positive-mass block of a nonrelativistic
expansion in odd kinetic/geometric scales relative to m
is D_+D_-/(2m), in addition to the supplied independent
Coulomb potential. This is the leading Pauli approximation
only: the higher relativistic potential commutators,
time-dependent FW terms, actual physical units and
domain/error bounds have not been paid.
At the initial G3 slice, phi=psi, gamma_J=psi² delta,
so b=eps B0/(4psi) in the marked orthonormal frame,
and psi=1+O(eps²) by P0T. Thus its linear term in eps is
the actual spatial source eps B0/4; the base metric and
its spatial connection differ from the flat control
only at quadratic order. At this order div B0=0 exactly.
The b² contribution is second order; do not use the
linear projection as a complete second-order Hamiltonian.

### BR170 — full selected n=2 shell, actual s/p interaction and signed triad

Take the original nonrelativistic hydrogenic n=2 shell
with both spin states retained and radial inverse Bohr
scale lambda>0 in the original dimensionless Hopf
length units (lambda is not inferred from gravity).
Real scalar orbitals:
 f_s=C(2-lambda r) exp(-lambda r/2),
 f_i=C lambda x_i exp(-lambda r/2), i=x,y,z,
 C²=lambda³/(32pi).
Both normalizations and angular moments verified.
Let zhat be the marked Hopf axis. Split original
 b=eps B0/4 into its parity even and odd parts:
 b_even=2eps[(1-r²)zhat+2z x]/(1+r²)^3,
 b_odd=4eps(zhat cross x)/(1+r²)^3.
The exact angular integration of the linear Pauli
operator gives two real coefficients
 a=eps lambda^5/(12m) int_0^infty
       r^5 exp(-lambda r)/(1+r²)^3 dr,
 c=eps lambda^5/(12m) int_0^infty
       r^4 exp(-lambda r)/(1+r²)^3 dr.
For a, the original signed radial integrand
 r²(3-r²-lambda r) exp(-lambda r)/(1+r²)^3
 is equal in integral to lambda r^5 exp(-lambda r)/(1+r²)^3
 by differentiating r³ exp(-lambda r)/(1+r²)^2;
 both endpoints vanish. Both a,c are nonzero with the
 sign of eps for m,lambda>0. No numerical quadrature
 or radial approximation of these coefficients required.

On the original complete eight spin-orbital basis
(s up/down, px up/down, py up/down, pz up/down),
the Hermitian first-order selected perturbation M has
 blocks:
 M_ss=0,
 M_si=-i a sigma dot(zhat cross e_i),
 M_is=M_si^dagger,
 M_ij=-i c sigma dot(e_i z_j-e_j z_i).
Thus the even Hopf component mixes s with transverse p;
the odd component transports p orbital-spin directions.
Exact characteristic polynomial and annihilator:
 det(E I-M)=E^4(E²-2a²-2c²)^2,
 M³=2(a²+c²) M.
For eps!=0 the rank is four; four zero modes and two
positive/two negative shifts. The spectral projectors
are Q0=I-M²/d², Q±=(M²/d²±M/d)/2, d=sqrt(2(a²+c²)).
This follows from the cubic, Hermiticity and trace;
not a separate native proof.
Under reversal eps -> -eps, M -> -M, Q+ and Q- exchange
for fixed d>0, zero support retained. At eps=0 M=0,
d=0 and the whole original shell reopens, not division
by zero. This is an actual selected atomic operator
realization of a signed triad (-,0,+) with multiplicities
(2,4,2) on the eight-state punctured address chart.
The four zero modes are not the missing ninth geometric
centre. The signed basis/orientation is essential;
the unordered energy spectrum forgets eps's sign.
These are shifts in the degenerate nonrelativistic
hydrogenic n=2 projection, not an actual measured
fine-structure spectrum. Fine structure, Lamb shift,
external fields, recoil and interactions lift the
degeneracy and must be combined as actual matrices
before diagonalization, not by adding unrelated spectra.
The leading 1/m approximation has no quantified
remainder here; no actual electron/nucleus source
or calibration implied by lambda or m.

### BR171 — exact compatible grazing traces and the boundary estimate cut

Use the original projected Weyl evolution w_t=A_i w_i,
not independent arbitrary subsidiary jets. In the
reference flat-principal frame, choose tangential direction
i=1, and Di=D_i[ids,:]X. The actual residual c=Di w
(up to the derivative/Fourier factor) satisfies
 K_i c=mu c whenever A_i w=mu w, with
 K_i=-D_i[ids,:] L. For each actual eigenspace the exact
matrix ranks are:
 mu=+/-1/2: dim 8 each, rank Di image 8,
             rank T Di on each 8, rank T_sp Di on each 2;
 mu=0: dim9, rank T Di 8, rank T_sp Di 1;
 mu=+/-1: dim5 each, both trace maps rank0.
The latter are the physical flat constraint-kernel
light-speed modes. This is an actual compatibility test,
not the abstract trace counterexample of BR150.
In particular the restricted spatial electric trace
DOES NOT annihilate the tangential half-speed branches.
Its normal-work factorization in BR143/146 therefore
does not by itself give a non-glancing estimate.

Analytical consequence for the uncoupled reference flat
bulk system on a full normal line, with periodic tangential
x1: SO(4) spatial covariance transports a chosen half-speed
eigenvector w0 with T_sp Di w0 nonzero to nearby normal
directions xi=(k,0,0,ell). Use the smoothly rotated
eigenvector w(xi) and residual polarization
 c(xi)=D(xi)X w(xi)/|xi|, with the selected independent
rows understood, so c -> Di w0 as ell/k ->0.
Use L²-normalized normal Fourier amplitude proportional
to k^-1/4 on |ell|<=sqrt(k), tangential frequency k.
For the half-speed dispersion omega=|xi|/2,
 |omega-k/2| <= ell²/(4k)<=1/4,
 so for 0<=t<=1 all phases relative to the central
frequency lie within 1/4 radian. At y=0 the projection
onto the fixed nonzero T_sp Di w0 therefore has magnitude
bounded below by a constant times k^1/4 for large k,
whereas the L² bulk residual norm stays bounded by
Plancherel and smooth polarization. One can choose
a smooth nonnegative compact frequency cutoff with the
same scaling; real data follow by pairing conjugate
frequencies. This written wave-packet argument proves
that no unrestricted same-order L² wall trace bound
follows from the flat compatible bulk evolution alone.
It is NOT a counterexample to the actual coupled
Einstein–Israel transmission/Cartan wall system:
the packet does not impose its jump, wall evolution
or corner data. Nor does it prove the optimal derivative
loss on solutions. The actual next cut is a joint
boundary-symbol estimate (glancing and zero modes
retained), using the signed energy cancellation and

### BR172 — simultaneous atomic operators, not independent spectra

On the original eight-state (s,px,py,pz) tensor spin basis,
take BR170's marked M(a,c), and add the selected central
s/p scalar energies and p-shell one-electron spin-orbit term:
 H=M+diag(Es I2,Ep I6)+zeta (0_2 direct_sum L dot S).
Here L_i[j,k]=-i epsilon_ijk and S_i=sigma_i/2, with the
BR163 radial coefficient as a possible leading source for
zeta (consistent energy units required). Es,Ep,zeta are
real supplied model coefficients, NOT an independently
derived Lamb shift or measured line. The direct matrix
identity [H,Jz]=0 holds with Jz=Lz+Sz, and the antiunitary
Theta=(I4 tensor i sigma_y) K (K complex conjugation)
satisfies Theta²=-I, Theta H Theta^-1=H and reverses Jz.
This is an exact symmetry of the selected initial matrix,
not a proof of time reversal of the full evolving metric
or independently applied magnetic Maxwell field.

The Jz=±3/2 spaces are one-dimensional with energy
 Ep+zeta/2. The Jz=±1/2 spaces are three-dimensional
and have the identical monic polynomial
 f(x)=(x-Es)[(x-Ep+zeta/2)(x-Ep)-2c²-zeta²/2]
             -2a²(x-Ep).
Consequently det(xI-H)=(x-Ep-zeta/2)² f(x)².
All four restrictions are checked by actual eigenspace
embeddings and matrix intertwining, plus exact charpoly.
This gives four Kramers pairs counted with multiplicity,
allowing further accidental degeneracy. It correctly
reduces to BR170's signed spectral triad when Es=Ep=0
and zeta=0. The rephased axial 120-degree spinor rotation
- exp(-2pi i Jz/3) has cube I and multiplicities (2,3,3),
with the two ±3/2 states in its invariant sector; this
follows directly from the four eigenspace dimensions,
not a new verified matrix exponential. This is an actual
selected atomic C3 action, distinct from the bare lane
cycle and from a full physical rotation's order six.

### BR173 — reverse solve the actual spectral observation

Assuming Es,Ep,zeta are known, the marked Jz=±1/2 cubic
can be used to recover squared Hopf matrix coefficients.
For Es!=Ep,
 a²=-f(Es)/(2(Es-Ep)),
 c²=-f(Ep)/(2(Ep-Es))-zeta²/4.
At Es=Ep the cubic reduces to
 (x-Ep)[(x-Ep+zeta/2)(x-Ep)-2(a²+c²)-zeta²/2],
so that the two independent algebraic coefficients
cannot be recovered individually from the spectrum;
the retained observation is a²+c². The original radial
integral relation may further constrain them, but that
is additional source information, not a spectral inverse.
The spectrum is even under simultaneous sign reversal,
even with the selected central splitting and spin-orbit
term included. A marked block measurement restores
the sign:
 a=(i/2)tr(sigma_y M_{s,px}),
 c=(i/2)tr(sigma_x M_{px,pz}).
The inverse is a mathematical observer of the projected
matrix, not a demonstrated experimental tomography protocol.
At a=c=0 retain the whole original shell, not an artificial
sign. On any observation quotient keep the source-specific
splitting and marked axes; this is not a universal spectral
reconstruction or canonical Leech correspondence.
Further relativistic, many-electron, nuclear, field,
curvature and temporal corrections remain necessary.

In the normal/tangential plane use the exact rational

 A(u)=cos(theta) A_1+sin(theta) A_n,

 (D is restricted to the BR143 independent rows).

 w_0=2u,w_4=-2u,w_8=u²-1,w_13=2(u²-1),

 w^T G w=24(1+u²)², and
 ||T_sp D(u) X w||²/(w^T G w)

where the D restriction is understood and T_sp is

since |xi| sin(theta)=ell and |cos(theta)|<=1.
The last bound is relative to the Weyl amplitude, not

### BR175 — nonzero-shape reference bulk/shape transmission splits exactly

Use a wall at y=0, plus side y>0 and minus side y<0
(the two one-sided collar coordinates of the original wall).
For the frozen reference differential system
 w_t=sum A_i partial_i w+A_n partial_y w,
 [w]=j Jm, m_t=sum B_i partial_i m-R mean(w),
 retain all 35 Weyl and nine shape coordinates, j<0.
The normal reflection F multiplies each packed Riemann
entry by (-1) to the number of normal indices. Actual
carrier construction verifies F²=I, F^T G F=G,
 F A_i=A_i F (tangential i), F A_n=-A_n F,
 FJ=J, RF=-R. P+=(I+F)/2 has rank19, P- rank16.
Set u=w+(y), v=F w-(-y) on y>0, a=(u+v)/2,
 b=(u-v)/2. Both a,b obey the same reference bulk
 differential equation. Full jump is
 w+-w-=2P- a+2P+ b,
 R mean(w)=R b.
Since J has even parity, the full shape-coupled system
 separates into
 a sector: P- a=0 at wall;
 b,m sector: P+ b=j Jm/2,
              m_t=sum B_i partial_i m-R b.
This is an exact split including m, not an assumption
that wall shape vanishes in all solutions. It includes
zero normal components and all original jump rows.
It does not assert independent surjectivity of all
boundary data or strong well-posedness for this over-
specified boundary formulation.
With S=G A_n, P±^T S P±=0 and S J=3 R^T H.
The a sector has zero normal work; b's work at wall
 is (1/2)b^T S b=(3j/2)m^T H R b.
Thus the half-line energy for b,m has positive
wall weight -3j/2 (with the usual 1/2 factor in
each quadratic energy); derivative of wall energy
 cancels the lower-end bulk flux exactly. Same identity
for complex modes with real-part Hermitian pairing.
For a sufficiently regular decaying homogeneous solution
of the reference Laplace-Fourier boundary problem with
Re s>0, real-part energy integration excludes nonzero
solutions (assuming no remote-boundary input), including
nonzero m; this is uniqueness only, not a resolvent
surjectivity or uniform trace estimate. At Re s=0
neutral modes are not excluded.
This is the full reference BULK/SHAPE subsystem split,
not the full curved Cartan and lower-order nonzero-
tension boundary system. In particular the actual
connection Kext terms, Ricci/Torsion defects, wall
metric matching and higher compatibility are not
removed by reference reflection.

Written same-order counterexamples for this reference
bulk/shape subsystem, not the full curved system:
use a periodic normal coordinate of length L (and
the frozen tangential Fourier chart), normal frequencies
ell=2pi n/L, k>0 integer tangential frequency.
For each positive ell choose w_mu(theta), theta=atan(ell/k)
 and its reflected partner at -ell. Extend periodically.
At the seam both partners' exponentials equal one;
the reference reflection-even sector obeys the boundary
condition with m=0, and magnetic traces vanish as above.
Normalize Weyl amplitudes by 1/|xi| (so the derivative
residual has bounded polarization), then by N^-1/2
for N chosen positive normal frequencies. By orthogonality
the bulk L² subsidiary norm stays uniformly bounded
(normalization on fixed volume); tangential H^s weights
multiply both sides by comparable k^s.
For half-speed choose 0<ell<=sqrt(k), N proportional
to sqrt(k). Frequencies omega=|xi|/2 obey
 0<=omega-k/2<=1/4, so the selected trace components
cohere over 0<=t<=1, up to central phase. Their
wall norm grows at least as k^(1/4). For stationary
choose 0<ell<=k/2, N proportional to k, mu=0.
There is no temporal dephasing, and cos²(theta)>=4/5,
so the trace grows at least as k^(1/2) for the same
bounded bulk residual norm. The complex representation
can be made real by adding conjugate modes, with the
same norm ratio. This proves sharp half-order necessity
in this particular frozen bulk/shape stationary trace
test, consistent with BR150's mixed-norm bound. It
does not prove sharpness for the full curved IBVP.
Ordinary time integration can gain a tangential order
for the half-speed packet (frequencies comparable to k)
but not for the stationary packet. Therefore simply
integrating the BR142 electric source in time is not
a uniform same-order remedy absent actual tangential
Cartan constraint/control. Need either the full actual
constraint exclusion of this even stationary family or
an augmented energy with correct surface/normal weights
that pays the half-order cost, not another bulk-only
trace or single incident mode estimate. BR164 derivative-
free bulk sources remain valid. The full nonzero-shape
reference split is paid; actual curved Cartan wall
system still open. The packet bounds are written analytic
arguments from exact matrices, not simulated packets.

### BR177 — original atomic radial relation closes a scalar inverse

Use BR170's actual selected a,c, with positive supplied
m and lambda (inverse Bohr scale in source length units).
Define I_j(lambda)=int_0^infty r^(4+j) exp(-lambda r)
 /(1+r²)^3 dr, for lambda>0.
Then a=eps lambda^5 I_1/(12m), c=eps lambda^5 I_0/(12m).
For nonzero eps the positive ratio
 rho(lambda)=a/c=I_1/I_0
 is a mean radius in the probability weight proportional
to r^4 exp(-lambda r)/(1+r²)^3. By dominated differentiation
on compact positive lambda intervals, I'_j=-I_(j+1).
Hence
 rho' = -(I_0 I_2-I_1²)/I_0² = -Var_lambda(r)<0.
Strictness follows from positive weight on an interval,
not a point measure. At lambda down to zero, I_0 tends
to 3pi/16 by dominated convergence, whereas I_1 tends
to infinity by monotone convergence (tail ~1/r).
At lambda up to infinity, substitute x=lambda r and
apply dominated convergence to the rescaled moments:
 lambda rho -> 5!/4!=5, so rho ->0.
Thus rho:(0,infty)->(0,infty) is a continuous strictly
decreasing bijection, with smooth inverse by rho'<0.
Integration by parts also gives
 lambda rho=5-6 E_lambda[r²/(1+r²)]<5.
These are written analytical arguments; the checker
verifies moment identities, normalization integral
and exact asymptotic moment ratio, not a numerical inverse.
For the BR173 nondegenerate known baseline Es!=Ep,
if both recovered a²,c² are positive, lambda is uniquely
determined from sqrt(a²/c²). Then
 |eps|=12m |c|/(lambda^5 I_0(lambda)).
A marked observation of either nonzero block restores
the sign of eps. This only recovers the original selected
model scale if m and units are known; baseline coefficients
may themselves be radius-dependent in a physical clock.
No fixed baseline physical inverse asserted without
calibration or the full matter model.
At Es=Ep the spectrum supplies a²+c² only. If lambda
is known from the independently supplied atomic radial
source then |eps| is recovered by
 12m sqrt(a²+c²)/(lambda^5 sqrt(I_0²+I_1²)).
If lambda is unknown, a continuous family of (lambda,|eps|)
remains at fixed positive norm; source relation alone
does not remove that degeneracy. At eps=0, both couplings
vanish and lambda cannot be recovered from them.
Retain the zero fibre, marked orientation and actual
baseline. No measured spectral data or detector precision
claim, no physical hydrogenic units inferred from 369.
This is the source-specific radial BIDI completion of
the selected BR170/173 observation, not full atomic CI,
relativistic or interacting spectrum.
